The first medium-term programme for 2006-2008 in 2005 was prepared in two documents, as well as the Ministry of Finance, for the powers of the suspended State Planning Agency. If the process is to be done with transparency, we consider it a sound decision to reduce the documents to one, but we're against the possibility of removing information from the mid-term financial programme by hiding behind the combining of documents. I'd also like to point out that we'll be in pursuit of such an initiative as the Good Party. On the other hand, mid-term financial plan and programme documents begin the budget process. The release of a document that initiated the budget process by the end of the year in September or October is both a big conflict and a big mistake. In the developed countries, the budget processes of the new year begin in April, May. According to current legislation, the mid-term financial plan, as part of the budget work, must be prepared and sent by September 15th, as well as in the first week of September, each year, but this programme and plans have not been prepared in time for many years. At the end of September, there were years when it was shared in early October. Moreover, the budget law proposal must be submitted to the Turkish Grand National Assembly on October 17th at the latest. In this framework, the relevant public institutions, their institutions, have to prepare their budgets exponentially before they receive a medium-term programme and a medium-term financial plan. In addition, assuming that the data presented in the budget proposal on October 17, 2020, for macroeconomic indicators of 2021, is valid for a full year, is to bury your head in the sand. The lack of revision of macroeconomic targets set for a year is an unequivocal practice in the world. The whole world reiterates its goals in September, before the new year begins, in the first half of the new year, the update of macroeconomic indicators by the name ▪ and there is no document available for updates in our country. I'm afraid there's no official update based on data. Reviews conducted based solely on statements. Such regulations are not acceptable in modern countries. For these reasons, the preparation of the central management budget should begin with the release of the mid-term programme adopted by the President in the Official Gazette by the end of May. The medium-term programme must include data for the next three years, in line with development plans, strategic plans and the requirements of general economic conditions. The main economic sizes of macropolitics, principles, goals and indicators must be recorded. Total income, expenses, balance and debt estimates must be shared as of budget types. Public administration's grant bid ceilings must be specified. The policies, principles, macroeconomic indicators that the government will implement in detail the first year in the new three years, and the remaining two in general should be known by institutions. Corporates should offer budgets through their funding ceilings. I ask that the budget of our nation's taxes be prepared with a much more responsible, conscious and caring attitude, and I salute our General Assembly.
